000028AC                    ;
000028AC                    ; New real VU code 18/4/2001
000028AC                    ;
000028AC                    ; This code calculates the VU levels by examining the samples QTM is currently playing
000028AC                    ; rather than scanning the RISC OS DMA buffer data and calculating the levels from there.
000028AC                    ;
000028AC                    ; The new routine produces identical output to scanning the DMA buffer (on 8bit sound comps)
000028AC                    ; however now works on RiscPCs with 16bit sound, which do not have an 8bit sound buffer!
000028AC                    ;
000028AC          
000028AC          
000028AC          .realVUs  ;new realVUs code (gives correct result on 16bit sound)
000028AC E92D1FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12}                   ;r14 stored already
000028B0 E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
000028B4 E5CC0018           STRB    R0,[R12,#vuflags]
000028B8          
000028B8          
000028B8 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
000028BC E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
000028C0 E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
000028C4          
000028C4          
000028C4 E5992028           LDR     R2,[R9,#vuheights]              ;old heights for channels 1-4
000028C8 E3A03000           MOV     R3,#0                           ;new heights for channels 1-4, 5-8
000028CC E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0
000028D0          .realvu_channelloop 
000028D0 EBFFFFFE           BL      realvu_chanctrl                 ;returns R0=new average vu height for this channel
000028D4 E20210FF           AND     R1,R2,#&FF                      ;old average vu height for this channel
000028D8 E0811000           ADD     R1,R1,R0
000028DC E18330A1           ORR     R3,R3,R1,LSR#1                  ;put averaged value in R3
000028E0 E1A03463           MOV     R3,R3,ROR#8
000028E4 E1A02462           MOV     R2,R2,ROR#8
000028E8          
000028E8          
000028E8 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
000028EC E2866001           ADD     R6,R6,#1
000028F0 E3360004           TEQ     R6,#4
000028F4 05893028           STREQ   R3,[R9,#vuheights]              ;store new heights for channels 1-4
000028F8 03A03000           MOVEQ   R3,#0
000028FC 0599202C           LDREQ   R2,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;old heights for channels 5-8
00002900 E156000B           CMP     R6,R11
00002904 BAFFFFF1           BLT     realvu_channelloop
00002908          
00002908

00002910          
00002910          .realvu_chanctrl ;r6=channel-1 (0-7)
00002910 E92D5FFE           STMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,R14}       ;
00002914 E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
00002918 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
0000291C C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
00002920 C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
00002924 CAFFFFFE           BGT     realvu_blankchannel     ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
00002928          
00002928          
00002928 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
0000292C 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
00002930 DAFFFFFE           BLE     realvu_blankchannel     ;pitch is 0, so sample is blank
00002934          
00002934          
00002934          .realvu_chanrunning 
00002934 E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
00002938 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
0000293C 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
00002940 E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
00002944          
00002944          
00002944 E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
00002948 AAFFFFFE           BGE     realvu_blankchannel     ;channel is blank....
0000294C          
0000294C          
0000294C E3520B01           CMP     R2,#1024                        ;
00002950 B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)
00002954 B7972102           LDRLT   R2,[R7,R2,LSL#2]                ;load Arc pitch increment
00002958 ABFFFFFE           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
0000295C          
0000295C          
0000295C E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
00002960 B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002964          
00002964          
00002964                    OPT opt%
00002964 E1A00000           MOV R0,R0
00002968 E1A00000           MOV R0,R0
0000296C                    FNlong_adr("  ",10,loglintab)
0000296C E3A0B000           MOV     R11,#0
00002970 E3A0C080           MOV     R12,#128                ;get average real vu height from 128 bytes of DMA data
00002974          
00002974          
00002974          .realvu_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002974 E7D0E641           LDRB    R14,[R0,R1,ASR#12]      ;load a byte of sample data
00002978 E05EE003           SUBS    R14,R14,R3              ;scale for volume
0000297C C3CEE001           BICGT   R14,R14,#%00000001      ;make +ve log (=even no.s)
00002980 C7DAE00E           LDRGTB  R14,[R10,R14]           ;load +ve linear byte (0-127)
00002984 C08BB00E           ADDGT   R11,R11,R14             ;if>0 accumulate
00002988 E0911082           ADDS    R1,R1,R2,LSL#1          ;add pitch*2, have we ended? (offset becomes GE)?
0000298C A0511605           SUBGES  R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start (and check offset now ok)
00002990 A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if offset still -ve, get back to repeat start
00002994          
00002994          
00002994 E25CC002           SUBS    R12,R12,#2
00002998 CAFFFFF5           BGT     realvu_sampleloop       ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
0000299C          
0000299C          
0000299C                    ; now R11 has 64*bytes (0 to 127), so need to divide by 128 (= >>7)to get value 0-63
0000299C          
0000299C          
0000299C E1A003AB           MOV     R0,R11,LSR#7            ;return R0=average vu level (0-64) for this DMA fill
000029A0          
000029A0          
000029A0                    ;ADD     R0,R0,R0,LSR#2          ;**old** linear scale-up so the vu fills 0-64 better
000029A0                    ;AND     R0,R0,#63               ; (..this on does 0-80, then clips down to 0-63!)
000029A0          
000029A0          
000029A0 E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;better log sq'd scale-up routine (T's ;-)
000029A4 E1A01000           MOV     R1,R0                   ;
000029A8 E0000091           MUL     R0,R1,R0                ;
000029AC E1A00320           MOV     R0,R0,LSR#6             ;
000029B0 E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;this does 0-64 on a log (sq'd) curve, so low vols are more amp'd
000029B4 E1500000           CMP     R0,R0 ; Clear V
000029B8 E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}
000029BC          
000029BC          
000029BC          .realvu_blankchannel 
000029BC E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
000029C0 E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}        ;exit with R0=vu height for this channel
000029C4          
000029C4          
000029C4                    ;
000029C4                    ; SWI QTM_DMABuffer and fake DMA Buffer creation routine for 16bit sound RiscPCs
000029C4                    ;
000029C4          
000029C4          
000029C4          .swi_qtmdmabuffer 
000029C4 E59C0078           LDR     R0,[R12,#fake8bitDMA]
000029C8 E3300000           TEQ     R0,#0
000029CC 059C00BC           LDREQ   R0,[R12,#DMABuffer]
000029D0 01A0F00E           MOVEQ   PC,R14
000029D4          
000029D4          
000029D4 E92D4000           STMFD   R13!,{R14}
000029D8 EBFFFFFE           BL      calcscope               ;update fake DMA block for 16bit-sound RiscPCs
000029DC E8BD8000           LDMFD   R13!,{PC}
000029E0          
000029E0          
000029E0          .calcscope ;called by SWI QTM_DMABuffer on 16bit sound RiscPCs
000029E0 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}               ;to generate a fake 8bit sound DMA buffer for progs use!
000029E4 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
000029E8 E599C078           LDR     R12,[R9,#fake8bitDMA]           ;R12=fake DMA address
000029EC          
000029EC          
000029EC E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
000029F0 E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
000029F4 E08CA20B           ADD     R10,R12,R11,LSL#4
000029F8 E08AA30B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#6               ;v1.45
000029FC E08AA38B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#7               ;***** fake DMA is always 208 bytes * no. of channels
00002A00          
00002A00          
00002A00 E1A0600B           MOV     R6,R11
00002A04          .calcscope_channelloop 
00002A04 EBFFFFFE           BL      calcscope_chanctrl
00002A08          
00002A08          
00002A08 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
00002A0C E28CC001           ADD     R12,R12,#1
00002A10 E2566001           SUBS    R6,R6,#1
00002A14 CAFFFFFA           BGT     calcscope_channelloop
00002A18 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00002A1C          
00002A1C          
00002A1C          .calcscope_chanctrl 
00002A1C E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}       ;
00002A20 E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
00002A24 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
00002A28 C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
00002A2C C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
00002A30 CAFFFFFE           BGT     calcscope_blankchannel  ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
00002A34          
00002A34          
00002A34 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
00002A38 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
00002A3C DAFFFFFE           BLE     calcscope_blankchannel

00002A70          .calcscope_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002A70 E7D06641           LDRB    R6,[R0,R1,ASR#12]       ;load a byte of sample data
00002A74 E0566003           SUBS    R6,R6,R3                ;scale for volume

00002A94          .calcscope_blankchannel

00002A98          .calcscope_clear ;

00002A9C E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;
00002AA0 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002AA4 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002AA8 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002AAC B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002AB0 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002AB4 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;only using 4 blankings per loop...
00002AB8 BAFFFFF6           BLT     calcscope_clear         ;...cleared rest of channel
00002ABC E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}        ;exit

00003674 E92D0003           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R1}           ;stack r0,r1
00003678 E89C0003           LDMIA   R12,{R0-R1}            ;r0=CIA delay, r1=CIA counter
0000367C E2511801           SUBS    R1,R1,#1<<16           ;take 1 lump of accurate time off CIA counter
00003680 D0811000           ADDLE   R1,R1,R0               ;if counter<=0 then add total CIA delay to it
00003684 E58C1004           STR     R1,[R12,#CIAcounter]   ;store CIA counter
00003688 E8BD0003           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R1}           ;reload r0,r1
0000368C C1A0F00E           MOVGT   PC,R14                 ;if CIA counter didn't run out, then exit

00003690          .ControlSong 
00003690 E92D4FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R11,R14}
00003694 E5DC0009           LDRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;load speed counter

0000369C D5DC0008           LDRLEB  R0,[R12,#speed]         ;if counter<=0 then reload it with the speed
000036A0 E5CC0009           STRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;store counter
000036A4 CAFFFFFE           BGT     doFX                    ;if counter didn't get to 0, then go and do the effects...
000036A8

00003F90 E2200001           EOR     R0,R0,#1                ;invert bit 0 = change sign!
00003F94 E5C10000           STRB    R0,[R1]
00003F98 E8BD8007           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,PC}
00003F9C                    ; - this very rare effect inverts actual sample data (in memory),
00003F9C                    ;   and is the only effect NOT included in QTM<v1.48. Although I
00003F9C                    ;   coded the routine, I never found a song which uses it, until
00003F9C                    ;   4mat provided a test track ;) thanks! Now fully implememnted.
00003F9C                    ;                                                             SH

00004264          .do_tremolo ; ***** 07 - tremolo *****
00004264 E5DB000F           LDRB    R0,[R11,#currentvol]
00004268 E59B1028           LDR     R1,[R11,#tremolo_speed] ;0-3 depth, 4-7 speed, 8-9 wave, 10=no reset, 26-30 pos, 31 sign
0000426C E201331F           AND     R3,R1,#&1F<<26          ;=&7C<<24 ;get pos in bits 26-30 (bit 31 is sign, so clear it)
00004270 E3110C03           TST     R1,#%11<<8
00004274                    ;ADDEQ   R4,R12,#(vibratotable-song_data) AND &FF00
00004274                    ;ADDEQ   R4,R4,#(vibratotable-song_data) AND &00FF
00004274 024F4038           ADREQ   R4,vibratotable_new
00004278 07D43D23           LDREQB  R3,[R4,R3,LSR#26]       ;not ASR, as b31 just used to flag add/sub value
0000427C 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     trem_set                ;PT prioritises 0/4 (sine)
00004280          
00004280          
00004280 E3110C01           TST R1,#%01<<8                  ;...then 1/5 (sawtooth)
00004284 1AFFFFFE           BNE trem_rampdown
00004288 E3A030FF           MOV R3,#255 ;1 seems to work with 4mat test track  ;...then 2/6 (sqr, and 3/7 treated as 2/6)
0000428C          .trem_set 
0000428C E201400F           AND     R4,R1,#&0F              ;depth
00004290 E0030394           MUL     R3,R4,R3                ;depth*value
00004294 E21124FF           ANDS    R2,R1,#&FF000000        ;R2=bits 26-30 pos (+b31 sign) (b24 & b25=0, because pos 26-30)
00004298 50800323           ADDPL   R0,R0,R3,LSR#6
0000429C 40500323           SUBMIS  R0,R0,R3,LSR#6
000042A0 43A00000           MOVMI   R0,#0
000042A4 E3500040           CMP     R0,#64
000042A8 C3A00040           MOVGT   R0,#64
000042AC          
000042AC          
000042AC E5CA000C           STRB    R0,[R10,#DMA_volume]
000042B0          
000042B0          
000042B0 E20110F0           AND     R1,R1,#&F0       ;=speed in bits %11110000
000042B4 E0822B01           ADD     R2,R2,R1,LSL#(24-2) ;add speed to pos (bits 26-30 + b31 sign)
000042B8 E1A02C42           MOV     R2,R2,ASR#24     ;keep sign, move to bits 0-7 (v1.49 fix - vibrato was fixed in 1.46a!)
000042BC E5CB202B           STRB    R2,[R11,#tremolo_pos]

00005494          .loglintab ;this is precalculated and loaded from a data file at assembly
00005594                    OPT opt%
00005594          
00005594                    ;
00005594                    ; Ultra compact pattern-shuffle, (c) STEP Harrison 2013
00005594                    ;
00005594                    ; uses precalculated table (64 bytes) and 80 bytes of code = 144 bytes total
00005594                    ; in order to reshuffle 2048 bytes of pattern data using no additional memory
00005594                    ;
00005594                    ; input pattern format c1-4 events 0,1,2...63 then c5-8 events 0,1,2...63
00005594                    ; output pattern format c1-8 events 0,1,2,3....61,62,63
00005594                    ;
00005594                    ; shuffle example input  0,1,2,3,4,5.....124,125,126,127
00005594                    ;         example output 0,64,1,65,2,66,3,67,4....125,62,126,63,127
00005594                    ;
00005594                    ; shuffle table calculated by hand/using spreadsheet...is there a better way?
00005594                    ;
00005594          
00005594          .shufflepattern ;reorder StarTrekker patters into FastTracker format
00005594 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}   ;R0=start addr of 8 channel pattern to reshuffe
00005598 E24F2008           ADR     R2,shuffletable     ;(alternate one offset from table, one from R1)
0000559C          
0000559C                    ;if table byte=0, skip, if table byte=255 end
0000559C E2801B01           ADD     R1,R0,#64*16        ;(64 to 127, use inbetween table bytes)
000055A0 E1A03000           MOV     R3,R0               ;output address
000055A4          .shuffleloop 
000055A4 E4D24001           LDRB    R4,[R2],#1          ;load table byte
000055A8 E3340000           TEQ     R4,#0               ;0=skip
000055AC 02833010           ADDEQ   R3,R3,#1*16         ;get to next event position
000055B0 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     skipthisshuffle
000055B4          
000055B4          
000055B4 E33400FF           TEQ     R4,#255             ;255=exit
000055B8 08BD9FFF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000055BC          
000055BC          
000055BC E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;load 4 channels from current event
000055C0 E0804204           ADD     R4,R0,R4,LSL#4
000055C4 E8941E00           LDMIA   R4,{R9,R10,R11,R12} ;load 4 channels from destination event
000055C8 E88401E0           STMIA   R4,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
000055CC E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12};swap them round and increment to next event
000055D0          .skipthisshuffle 
000055D0 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;...do the next event using R1 as swap address
000055D4 E8911E00           LDMIA   R1,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
000055D8 E8A101E0           STMIA   R1!,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
000055DC E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
000055E0 EAFFFFEF           B       shuffleloop         ;keep going until end of table
000055E4          
000055E4          
000055E4          .shuffletable 
000055E4 00                 DCB 0  ;1
000055E5 40                 DCB 64

00005624                    FNassm_initsong ;QTM_Song
00005624                    OPT opt%
00005624          
00005624                    ; on entry
00005624          
00005624                    ;          R0          \  sample end address            - sample end address
00005624                    ;          R1           } -ve sample offset             - -ve sample offset
00005624                    ;          R2           } (amiga type) pitch            - sample pitch increment (Arc)
00005624                    ;          R3           } linear volume (0-64)          - sample volume (0-255)
00005624                    ;          R4           } -ve repeat offset             - -ve repeat offset
00005624                    ;          R5          /                                - load/store sample byte
00005624                    ;          R6      -ve if channel handler changed       - channel counter (0->channel-1)
00005624                    ;          R7      <unknown>                            -
00005624                    ;          R8      uS <? - unknown>                     - ptr to 'DMA_blocks'
00005624                    ;          R9      ptr to initblock                     - ptr to 'song_data' (usually R12)
00005624                    ;          R10     DMA end                              - DMA end
00005624                    ;          R11     DMA gap                              - DMA gap
00005624                    ;          R12     DMA start                            - DMA ptr
00005624                    ;          R13     stack                                - stack
00005624                    ;          R14     exit                                 - end
00005624

0000568C E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
00005690 C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
00005694 C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)

00002364          .tmr_setlinetime 
00002364                    ;
00002364                    ; R9  s=speed
00002364                    ; R10 c=CIA tempo
00002364                    ;
00002364                    ; equation; time per line=10s/4c (*65536 for accuracy)
00002364                    ;
00002364 E92D4003           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R1,R14}
00002368 E3A0090A           MOV     R0,#&28000
0000236C E0000099           MUL     R0,R9,R0
00002370 E1A0100A           MOV     R1,R10
00002374 EB0010B7           BL      r0_div_r1               ;do (10s<<16)/4c - result in r0<<16
00002378 E1A08000           MOV     R8,R0
0000237C E8BD8003           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R1,PC}
00002380          
00002380          
00002380                    ; R12=song_data
00002380                    ; R11=address of current word
00002380                    ; R10=CIA tempo (32-255)
00002380                    ; R9=speed (1-31)
00002380                    ; R8=time per line = (10*speed/4*cia tempo)*65536
00002380                    ; R7=time so far
00002380                    ; R6=channel-1 (0-4/6/8)
00002380                    ; R5=current word
00002380                    ; R4=sequence
00002380                    ; R3=event
00002380          
00002380

00002760 E92D003F           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R5}
00002764 E5DC0018           LDRB    R0,[R12,#vuflags]
00002768 E5DC101A           LDRB    R1,[R12,#vugravity]
0000276C E59C2028           LDR     R2,[R12,#vuheights]
00002770 E3A0E008           MOV     R14,#8
00002774          .fakevuloop 
00002774 E1A05C22           MOV     R5,R2,LSR#24
00002778 E1A04822           MOV     R4,R2,LSR#16
0000277C E20440FF           AND     R4,R4,#&FF
00002780 E1A03422           MOV     R3,R2,LSR#8
00002784 E20330FF           AND     R3,R3,#&FF
00002788 E20220FF           AND     R2,R2,#&FF
0000278C          
0000278C          
0000278C E3100001           TST     R0,#%1<<0       ;1st/5th chan
00002790 13A02040           MOVNE   R2,#64
00002794 00422001           SUBEQ   R2,R2,R1
00002798 E3520000           CMP     R2,#0
0000279C 43A02000           MOVMI   R2,#0
000027A0 E3100002           TST     R0,#%1<<1       ;2nd/6th chan
000027A4 13A03040           MOVNE   R3,#64
000027A8 00433001           SUBEQ   R3,R3,R1
000027AC E3530000           CMP     R3,#0
000027B0 C1822403           ORRGT   R2,R2,R3,LSL#8
000027B4 E3100004           TST     R0,#%1<<2       ;3rd/7th chan
000027B8 13A04040           MOVNE   R4,#64
000027BC 00444001           SUBEQ   R4,R4,R1
000027C0 E3540000           CMP     R4,#0
000027C4 C1822804           ORRGT   R2,R2,R4,LSL#16
000027C8 E3100008           TST     R0,#%1<<3       ;4th/8th chan
000027CC 13A05040           MOVNE   R5,#64
000027D0 00455001           SUBEQ   R5,R5,R1
000027D4 E3550000           CMP     R5,#0
000027D8 C1822C05           ORRGT   R2,R2,R5,LSL#24
000027DC          
000027DC          
000027DC E25EE008           SUBS    R14,R14,#8
000027E0 A58C2028           STRGE   R2,[R12,#vuheights]
000027E4 A3A02000           MOVGE   R2,#0
000027E8 A5CC2018           STRGEB  R2,[R12,#vuflags]
000027EC A59C202C           LDRGE   R2,[R12,#vuheights58]
000027F0 A1A00220           MOVGE   R0,R0,LSR#4
000027F4 AAFFFFDE           BGE     fakevuloop
000027F8 E58C202C           STR     R2,[R12,#vuheights58]
000027FC E8BD803F           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R5,PC}
00002800

00002800          .effectVUs ;r14 stored, r12=song_data

00002818 E28C3028           ADD     R3,R12,#vuheights
0000281C E3A04000           MOV     R4,#0
00002820          .seteffectVUs 
00002820 E7D35004           LDRB    R5,[R3,R4]
00002824 E1100412           TST     R0,R2,LSLR4
00002828 15DB500F           LDRNEB  R5,[R11,#currentvol]
0000282C 1A000015           BNE     doneeffectVU            ;note just played, so set to current volume
00002830          
00002830          
00002830 E5DB601D           LDRB    R6,[R11,#currentFXnum]
00002834 E3560005           CMP     R6,#&5
00002838 13560006           CMPNE   R6,#&6
0000283C 1356000A           CMPNE   R6,#&A
00002840 1356000C           CMPNE   R6,#&C
00002844 05DB500F           LDREQB  R5,[R11,#currentvol]    ;any of those effects, then set height to current volume
00002848 0A00000E           BEQ     doneeffectVU
0000284C E3560007           CMP     R6,#&7                  ;volume wobble? - set to actual (DMA) volume
00002850 05DA500C           LDREQB  R5,[R10,#DMA_volume]    ;fixed 29/6/97 (applied 16/4/01!)-didn't read from correct DMA addr
00002854 0A00000B           BEQ     doneeffectVU
00002858          
00002858          
00002858 E356000E           CMP     R6,#&E
0000285C 1A000007           BNE     graveffectVU            ;VU not affected by any effects...
00002860 E5DB601E           LDRB    R6,[R11,#currentFXdata]
00002864 E1A06226           MOV     R6,R6,LSR#4
00002868 E356000A           CMP     R6,#&A
0000286C 1356000B           CMPNE   R6,#&B
00002870 1356000C           CMPNE   R6,#&C
00002874 1356000D           CMPNE   R6,#&D
00002878 05DB500F           LDREQB  R5,[R11,#currentvol]
0000287C 0A000001           BEQ     doneeffectVU
00002880          
00002880          
00002880          .graveffectVU 
00002880 E0555001           SUBS    R5,R5,R1
00002884 43A05000           MOVMI   R5,#0
00002888          .doneeffectVU 
00002888 E7C35004           STRB    R5,[R3,R4]
0000288C E2844001           ADD     R4,R4,#1
00002890 E28BB040           ADD     R11,R11,#64
00002894 E28AA018           ADD     R10,R10,#24
00002898 E3540008           CMP     R4,#8
0000289C BAFFFFDF           BLT     seteffectVUs
000028A0          
000028A0          
000028A0 E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
000028A4 E5CC0018           STRB    R0,[R12,#vuflags]
000028A8 E8BD8C7F           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R6,R10,R11,PC}
000028AC          
000028AC          
000028AC                    ;
000028AC                    ; New real VU code 18/4/2001
000028AC                    ;
000028AC                    ; This code calculates the VU levels by examining the samples QTM is currently playing
000028AC                    ; rather than scanning the RISC OS DMA buffer data and calculating the levels from there.
000028AC                    ;
000028AC                    ; The new routine produces identical output to scanning the DMA buffer (on 8bit sound comps)
000028AC                    ; however now works on RiscPCs with 16bit sound, which do not have an 8bit sound buffer!
000028AC                    ;
000028AC          
000028AC          
000028AC          .realVUs  ;new realVUs code (gives correct result on 16bit sound)
000028AC E92D1FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12}                   ;r14 stored already
000028B0 E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
000028B4 E5CC0018           STRB    R0,[R12,#vuflags]
000028B8          
000028B8          
000028B8 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
000028BC E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
000028C0 E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
000028C4          
000028C4          
000028C4 E5992028           LDR     R2,[R9,#vuheights]              ;old heights for channels 1-4
000028C8 E3A03000           MOV     R3,#0                           ;new heights for channels 1-4, 5-8
000028CC E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0
000028D0          .realvu_channelloop 
000028D0 EB00000E           BL      realvu_chanctrl                 ;returns R0=new average vu height for this channel
000028D4 E20210FF           AND     R1,R2,#&FF                      ;old average vu height for this channel
000028D8 E0811000           ADD     R1,R1,R0
000028DC E18330A1           ORR     R3,R3,R1,LSR#1                  ;put averaged value in R3
000028E0 E1A03463           MOV     R3,R3,ROR#8
000028E4 E1A02462           MOV     R2,R2,ROR#8
000028E8          
000028E8          
000028E8 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
000028EC E2866001           ADD     R6,R6,#1
000028F0 E3360004           TEQ     R6,#4
000028F4 05893028           STREQ   R3,[R9,#vuheights]              ;store new heights for channels 1-4
000028F8 03A03000           MOVEQ   R3,#0
000028FC 0599202C           LDREQ   R2,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;old heights for channels 5-8
00002900 E156000B           CMP     R6,R11
00002904 BAFFFFF1           BLT     realvu_channelloop
00002908          
00002908          
00002908 E589302C           STR     R3,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;store new heights for channels 5-8
0000290C E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00002910          
00002910          
00002910          .realvu_chanctrl ;r6=channel-1 (0-7)
00002910 E92D5FFE           STMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,R14}       ;
00002914 E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
00002918 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
0000291C C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
00002920 C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
00002924 CA000024           BGT     realvu_blankchannel     ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
00002928          
00002928          
00002928 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
0000292C 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
00002930 DA000021           BLE     realvu_blankchannel     ;pitch is 0, so sample is blank
00002934          
00002934          
00002934          .realvu_chanrunning 
00002934 E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
00002938 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
0000293C 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
00002940 E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
00002944          
00002944          
00002944 E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
00002948 AA00001B           BGE     realvu_blankchannel     ;channel is blank....
0000294C          
0000294C          
0000294C E3520B01           CMP     R2,#1024                        ;
00002950 B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)
00002954 B7972102           LDRLT   R2,[R7,R2,LSL#2]                ;load Arc pitch increment
00002958 AB000DD5           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
0000295C          
0000295C          
0000295C E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
00002960 B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002964          
00002964          
00002964                    OPT opt%
00002964 E1A00000           MOV R0,R0
00002964                    OPT opt%
00002964 E28FA028           EQUD optcode1%
00002968 E28AAC2B           EQUD optcode2%
0000296C                    FNlong_adr("  ",10,loglintab)
0000296C E3A0B000           MOV     R11,#0
00002970 E3A0C080           MOV     R12,#128                ;get average real vu height from 128 bytes of DMA data
00002974          
00002974          
00002974          .realvu_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002974 E7D0E641           LDRB    R14,[R0,R1,ASR#12]      ;load a byte of sample data
00002978 E05EE003           SUBS    R14,R14,R3              ;scale for volume
0000297C C3CEE001           BICGT   R14,R14,#%00000001      ;make +ve log (=even no.s)
00002980 C7DAE00E           LDRGTB  R14,[R10,R14]           ;load +ve linear byte (0-127)
00002984 C08BB00E           ADDGT   R11,R11,R14             ;if>0 accumulate
00002988 E0911082           ADDS    R1,R1,R2,LSL#1          ;add pitch*2, have we ended? (offset becomes GE)?
0000298C A0511605           SUBGES  R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start (and check offset now ok)
00002990 A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if offset still -ve, get back to repeat start
00002994          
00002994          
00002994 E25CC002           SUBS    R12,R12,#2
00002998 CAFFFFF5           BGT     realvu_sampleloop       ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
0000299C          
0000299C          
0000299C                    ; now R11 has 64*bytes (0 to 127), so need to divide by 128 (= >>7)to get value 0-63
0000299C          
0000299C          
0000299C E1A003AB           MOV     R0,R11,LSR#7            ;return R0=average vu level (0-64) for this DMA fill
000029A0          
000029A0          
000029A0                    ;ADD     R0,R0,R0,LSR#2          ;**old** linear scale-up so the vu fills 0-64 better
000029A0                    ;AND     R0,R0,#63               ; (..this on does 0-80, then clips down to 0-63!)
000029A0          
000029A0          
000029A0 E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;better log sq'd scale-up routine (T's ;-)
000029A4 E1A01000           MOV     R1,R0                   ;
000029A8 E0000091           MUL     R0,R1,R0                ;
000029AC E1A00320           MOV     R0,R0,LSR#6             ;
000029B0 E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;this does 0-64 on a log (sq'd) curve, so low vols are more amp'd
000029B4 E1500000           CMP     R0,R0 ; Clear V
000029B8 E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}
000029BC          
000029BC          
000029BC          .realvu_blankchannel 
000029BC E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
000029C0 E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}        ;exit with R0=vu height for this channel
000029C4          
000029C4          
000029C4                    ;
000029C4                    ; SWI QTM_DMABuffer and fake DMA Buffer creation routine for 16bit sound RiscPCs
000029C4                    ;
000029C4          
000029C4          
000029C4          .swi_qtmdmabuffer 
000029C4 E59C0078           LDR     R0,[R12,#fake8bitDMA]
000029C8 E3300000           TEQ     R0,#0
000029CC 059C00BC           LDREQ   R0,[R12,#DMABuffer]
000029D0 01A0F00E           MOVEQ   PC,R14
000029D4          
000029D4          
000029D4 E92D4000           STMFD   R13!,{R14}
000029D8 EB000000           BL      calcscope               ;update fake DMA block for 16bit-sound RiscPCs
000029DC E8BD8000           LDMFD   R13!,{PC}
000029E0          
000029E0          
000029E0          .calcscope ;called by SWI QTM_DMABuffer on 16bit sound RiscPCs
000029E0 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}               ;to generate a fake 8bit sound DMA buffer for progs use!
000029E4 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
000029E8 E599C078           LDR     R12,[R9,#fake8bitDMA]           ;R12=fake DMA address
000029EC          
000029EC          
000029EC E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
000029F0 E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
000029F4 E08CA20B           ADD     R10,R12,R11,LSL#4
000029F8 E08AA30B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#6               ;v1.45
000029FC E08AA38B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#7               ;***** fake DMA is always 208 bytes * no. of channels
00002A00          
00002A00          
00002A00 E1A0600B           MOV     R6,R11
00002A04          .calcscope_channelloop 
00002A04 EB000004           BL      calcscope_chanctrl
00002A08          
00002A08          
00002A08 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
00002A0C E28CC001           ADD     R12,R12,#1
00002A10 E2566001           SUBS    R6,R6,#1
00002A14 CAFFFFFA           BGT     calcscope_channelloop

00002A24 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)

00002A2C C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)

00002A64 AB000D92           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
00002A68          
00002A68          
00002A68 E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
00002A6C B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002A70          
00002A70          
00002A70          .calcscope_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002A70 E7D06641           LDRB    R6,[R0,R1,ASR#12]       ;load a byte of sample data
00002A74 E0566003           SUBS    R6,R6,R3                ;scale for volume
00002A78 43A06000           MOVMI   R6,#0                   ;reset to 0 if vol too low
00002A7C E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;store in fake DMA block
00002A80 E0911002           ADDS    R1,R1,R2                ;have we ended (offset becomes GE)?
00002A84 A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start
00002A88          
00002A88          
00002A88 E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;check fake DMA end...
00002A8C BAFFFFF7           BLT     calcscope_sampleloop    ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
00002A90 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00002A94          
00002A94          
00002A94          .calcscope_blankchannel 
00002A94 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0                   ;
00002A98          .calcscope_clear ;
00002A98 E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A9C E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;
00002AA0 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002AA4 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002AA8 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002AAC B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002AB0 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002AB4 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;only using 4 blankings per loop...
00002AB8 BAFFFFF6           BLT     calcscope_clear         ;...cleared rest of channel
00002ABC E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}        ;exit

00003671 00                 EQUB    0
00003674                    ALIGN
00003674                    FNassm_mod      ;QTM_Mod  (incl. timer code and VU code)
00003674                    OPT opt%
00003674          
00003674                    ;  __________________________________________
00003674                    ; |                                          |\
00003674                    ; | QTM v1.49 (c) 1993-2013 Phoenix/Quantum  | |
00003674                    ; |__________________________________________| |
00003674                    ;  \__________________________________________\|
00003674                    ;
00003674          
00003674                    OPT opt%
00003674                    .CIAhandler                    ;r12 = song_data on entry
00003674 E92D0003           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R1}           ;stack r0,r1
00003678 E89C0003           LDMIA   R12,{R0-R1}            ;r0=CIA delay, r1=CIA counter
0000367C E2511801           SUBS    R1,R1,#1<<16           ;take 1 lump of accurate time off CIA counter
00003680 D0811000           ADDLE   R1,R1,R0               ;if counter<=0 then add total CIA delay to it
00003684 E58C1004           STR     R1,[R12,#CIAcounter]   ;store CIA counter
00003688 E8BD0003           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R1}           ;reload r0,r1
0000368C C1A0F00E           MOVGT   PC,R14                 ;if CIA counter didn't run out, then exit
00003690                    OPT opt%
00003690          
00003690          
00003690          .ControlSong 
00003690 E92D4FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R11,R14}
00003694 E5DC0009           LDRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;load speed counter
00003698 E2500001           SUBS    R0,R0,#1                ;take off 1 (can be <0 if speed 0 used to stop song?)
0000369C D5DC0008           LDRLEB  R0,[R12,#speed]         ;if counter<=0 then reload it with the speed
000036A0 E5CC0009           STRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;store counter
000036A4 CA0000B2           BGT     doFX                    ;if counter didn't get to 0, then go and do the effects...
000036A8          
000036A8          
000036A8 E5DC000A           LDRB    R0,[R12,#patterndelay]  ;load pattern delay time
000036AC E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;is it 0?
000036B0 1A0000AB           BNE     waitdelay               ;if not, then go to the delay effects/count down routine
000036B4          
000036B4          
000036B4          .GetNewNote 
000036B4 E5DC101B           LDRB    R1,[R12,#songstopflags] ;now all channels have been checked, test for stop-speed
000036B8 E3310000           TEQ     R1,#0
000036BC 1B000184           BLNE    stopflagset             ;returns if we don't stop
000036C0          
000036C0          
000036C0 E28CB00C           ADD     R11,R12,#songaddr       ;r11=addr of song address, then current event vars
000036C4 E89B0003           LDMIA   R11,{R0,R1}             ;r0=song address, r1=sequence (16-24) event (0-7)
000036C8 E2800FEE           ADD     R0,R0,#952              ;r0=sequence table address
000036CC E7D02821           LDRB    R2,[R0,R1,LSR#16]       ;r2=pattern
000036D0 E1811402           ORR     R1,R1,R2,LSL#8          ;r1=sequence (16-24) pattern (8-15) event (0-7)
000036D4 E58C1014           STR     R1,[R12,#lastevent]     ;store a copy of the position vars
000036D8 E20110FF           AND     R1,R1,#&FF              ;r1=event
000036DC          
000036DC          
000036DC E2808084           ADD     R8,R0,#132              ;add 132 to r0, to get to start of patterns, and put in r10
000036E0 E59C007C           LDR     R0,[R12,#patternlen]    ;v1.44 support for 8CHN and 6CHN
000036E4 E0288092           MLA     R8,R2,R0,R8             ;add current patt*patternlen to pattern start, gets to this pattern start
000036E8 E1A00320           MOV     R0,R0,LSR#6             ;patternlen/64 = event length (16/24/32 for 4, 8 FLT/6/8)
000036EC E0288091           MLA     R8,R1,R0,R8             ;add on current event*event length (no longer just 16)
000036F0 E58C8074           STR     R8,[R12,#tempaddrptr]
000036F4          
000036F4          
000036F4 E28CB080           ADD     R11,R12,#(channeldata-song_data)-64 ;r11=addr of channel blocks
000036F8 E3A09000           MOV     R9,#0                               ;r9=channel-1
000036FC          .dochannels 
000036FC E28CAD0B           ADD     R10,R12,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)     ;work out each time, incase of fake DMA!!!
00003700 E08AA209           ADD     R10,R10,R9,LSL#4
00003704 E08AA189           ADD     R10,R10,R9,LSL#3                    ;(channel DMA blocks are 24 bytes long)
00003708 E5DC0047           LDRB    R0,[R12,#songchans]  ;v1.44
0000370C E3300004           TEQ     R0,#4                ; |   if 4 channels
00003710 05DC0095           LDREQB  R0,[R12,#soundflags] ;v1.44  load sound flags
00003714 E3100020           TST     R0,#%1<<5            ; |   is b5 (play on c5-8) set? (songchans<32 chans, will be 0)
00003718 15DC0092           LDRNEB  R0,[R12,#chans_use]  ; |
0000371C 13300004           TEQNE   R0,#4                ; |  (***not checking for 8, but checking NOT 4!***)
00003720 128AA060           ADDNE   R10,R10,#4*24        ; |  if 'play on chan5-8' and 8 chans selected, do so! 28/6/96
00003724 E28BB040           ADD     R11,R11,#64          ;...now at this channel's 64 byte channel data block
00003728 E59C8074           LDR     R8,[R12,#tempaddrptr]
0000372C E7988109           LDR     R8,[R8,R9,LSL#2]     ;load note word for this channel (r8)
00003730          
00003730          
00003730 E59C0098           LDR     R0,[R12,#channelstate]                  ; *****              ** R8 must have note word
00003734 E3A01002           MOV     R1,#%10                                 ;      *****        **     by this point
00003738 E1100911           TST     R0,R1,LSLR9                             ;      *****    ** **
0000373C 028CAB01           ADDEQ   R10,R12,#(fakeDMAspace-song_data) AND &FF00      ; *****          ***
00003740 028AA028           ADDEQ   R10,R10,#(fakeDMAspace-song_data) AND &00FF      ; *****          ***
00003744 0A000056           BEQ     skipall                                 ;using fake DMA slot, so chan is left ok...
00003748          
00003748          
00003748 E3380000           TEQ     R8,#0                ;fix v1.46 18/1/22, only reset period for music
0000374C 1A000003           BNE     checksampleset       ;                   (v1.44/5 didn't reset any!)
00003750 E5DA000D           LDRB    R0,[R10,#DMA_sampleflag] ;R0=0 music sample, 255=sound sample
00003754 E33000FF           TEQ     R0,#255
00003758 1A000049           BNE     skipnote             ;skipnote resets period (needed for music FX)
0000375C EA000050           B       skipall              ;skipall doesn't reset period (use for sound sample)
00003760          
00003760          
00003760          .checksampleset 
00003760 E2080010           AND     R0,R8,#&00000010     ;upper 1 bit of sample number
00003764 E208160F           AND     R1,R8,#&00F00000     ;lower 4 bits of sample number
00003768 E1907A21           ORRS    R7,R0,R1,LSR#20      ;r7 = sample number
0000376C 0A00000C           BEQ     donesample           ;if sample number was 0, then skip sample setting bit...
00003770          
00003770          
00003770 E5CB701C           STRB    R7,[R11,#samplenumber]  ;store (new) sample number in channel's block
00003774          
00003774          
00003774 E2470001           SUB     R0,R7,#1                        ;r0=sample-1
00003778 E28C7D11           ADD     R7,R12,#(sampletable-song_data) ;get addr of sample data table in r5
0000377C E0877200           ADD     R7,R7,R0,LSL#4                  ;add (sample-1)*16 = correct info block for sample
00003780 E0877100           ADD     R7,R7,R0,LSL#2                  ;(+4) ******************* -(*20)

00003AAC 0A000012           BEQ     unknownFX               ;if not, skip it...

00003BDC                    ;TST     R8,#&00000007           ;v1.44 anything in upper 3 (was 2) bits of note period?
00003BDC                    ;TSTEQS  R8,#&0000FF00           ;no!, so anything in lower 8 bits of note period?...
00003BDC                    ;LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R7,PC}         ;still no! - so we're not going to set the offset!

00003BF8

00003BFC E58B2004           STR     R2,[R11,#smp_length]    ; } v1.47 bug fix [mod.way to rude, pos 32]

00003C04 C2633000           RSBGT   R3,R3,#0
00003C08 C0833A01           ADDGT   R3,R3,R1,LSL#8+12       ;if value was ok, get to new sample offset

00003F9D 05                 EQUB   5

00003FA5 13                 EQUB  19

00003FA7 1A                 EQUB  26
00003FA8 20                 EQUB  32
00003FA9 2B                 EQUB  43

00003FAB 80                 EQUB 128

00005390          .bytecompare 
00005390 E4D57001           LDRB    R7,[R5],#1
00005394 E3570000           CMP     R7,#0

0000539C E1540002           CMP     R4,R2           ;has the offset into the main string overflowed?

000053A4 E7D06004           LDRB    R6,[R0,R4]
000053A8 E3560041           CMP     R6,#ASC"A"      ; }
000053AC A2866020           ADDGE   R6,R6,#32       ; }_ set letters A-Z **ON LY**           *** FAST
000053B0 E356007A           CMP     R6,#ASC"Z"+32   ; }  to lower case (+32)                *** method!!!
000053B4 C2466020           SUBGT   R6,R6,#32       ; }
000053B8 E2844001           ADD     R4,R4,#1

000053C8 E1530002           CMP     R3,R2           ;run out of place to look?

00005494          .loglintab ;this is precalculated and loaded from a data file at assembly
00005594                    OPT opt%
00005594          
00005594                    ;
00005594                    ; Ultra compact pattern-shuffle, (c) STEP Harrison 2013
00005594                    ;
00005594                    ; uses precalculated table (64 bytes) and 80 bytes of code = 144 bytes total
00005594                    ; in order to reshuffle 2048 bytes of pattern data using no additional memory
00005594                    ;
00005594                    ; input pattern format c1-4 events 0,1,2...63 then c5-8 events 0,1,2...63
00005594                    ; output pattern format c1-8 events 0,1,2,3....61,62,63
00005594                    ;
00005594                    ; shuffle example input  0,1,2,3,4,5.....124,125,126,127
00005594                    ;         example output 0,64,1,65,2,66,3,67,4....125,62,126,63,127
00005594                    ;
00005594                    ; shuffle table calculated by hand/using spreadsheet...is there a better way?
00005594                    ;
00005594          
00005594          .shufflepattern ;reorder StarTrekker patters into FastTracker format
00005594 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}   ;R0=start addr of 8 channel pattern to reshuffe
00005598 E28F2044           ADR     R2,shuffletable     ;(alternate one offset from table, one from R1)
0000559C          
0000559C                    ;if table byte=0, skip, if table byte=255 end
0000559C E2801B01           ADD     R1,R0,#64*16        ;(64 to 127, use inbetween table bytes)
000055A0 E1A03000           MOV     R3,R0               ;output address
000055A4          .shuffleloop 
000055A4 E4D24001           LDRB    R4,[R2],#1          ;load table byte
000055A8 E3340000           TEQ     R4,#0               ;0=skip
000055AC 02833010           ADDEQ   R3,R3,#1*16         ;get to next event position
000055B0 0A000006           BEQ     skipthisshuffle
000055B4          
000055B4          
000055B4 E33400FF           TEQ     R4,#255             ;255=exit
000055B8 08BD9FFF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000055BC          
000055BC          
000055BC E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;load 4 channels from current event
000055C0 E0804204           ADD     R4,R0,R4,LSL#4
000055C4 E8941E00           LDMIA   R4,{R9,R10,R11,R12} ;load 4 channels from destination event
000055C8 E88401E0           STMIA   R4,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
000055CC E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12};swap them round and increment to next event
000055D0          .skipthisshuffle 
000055D0 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;...do the next event using R1 as swap address
000055D4 E8911E00           LDMIA   R1,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
000055D8 E8A101E0           STMIA   R1!,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
000055DC E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
000055E0 EAFFFFEF           B       shuffleloop         ;keep going until end of table
000055E4          
000055E4

00005624                    FNassm_initsong ;QTM_Song

00005624          
00005624                    ; on entry
00005624          
00005624                    ;          R0          \  sample end address            - sample end address
00005624                    ;          R1           } -ve sample offset             - -ve sample offset
00005624                    ;          R2           } (amiga type) pitch            - sample pitch increment (Arc)
00005624                    ;          R3           } linear volume (0-64)          - sample volume (0-255)
00005624                    ;          R4           } -ve repeat offset             - -ve repeat offset
00005624                    ;          R5          /                                - load/store sample byte
00005624                    ;          R6      -ve if channel handler changed       - channel counter (0->channel-1)
00005624                    ;          R7      <unknown>                            -
00005624                    ;          R8      uS <? - unknown>                     - ptr to 'DMA_blocks'
00005624                    ;          R9      ptr to initblock                     - ptr to 'song_data' (usually R12)
00005624                    ;          R10     DMA end                              - DMA end
00005624                    ;          R11     DMA gap                              - DMA gap
00005624                    ;          R12     DMA start                            - DMA ptr
00005624                    ;          R13     stack                                - stack
00005624                    ;          R14     exit                                 - end
00005624
